<margin>matheus</margin>

<image-desc>A full-page medieval illumination depicting Christ in Majesty (Majestas Domini). Christ is seated on a colorful, cushioned throne within a large, golden mandorla. He is shown with a cruciform halo, wearing a blue tunic and a reddish-brown mantle. His right hand is raised in a gesture of blessing, and his left hand holds an open book. His feet rest on a footstool. The background around the mandorla consists of a rectangular field with a blue and red checkered (diapered) pattern, with each square containing a small gold dot. In the four corners are circular medallions containing the symbols of the four Evangelists: an angel representing St. Matthew (top left), an eagle for St. John (top right), a winged lion for St. Mark (bottom left), and a winged ox for St. Luke (bottom right). Each medallion is accompanied by a name label for the respective Evangelist.</image-desc>

<language>Latin</language>
<page-type>illustration</page-type>
<page-num>9</page-num>

<image-desc>
A full-page medieval manuscript illumination on a gold leaf background, enclosed within an ornate red, blue, and white geometric border. The page depicts a series of animals organized into horizontal registers alongside a seated figure.

In the upper-left section, a figure (likely the Creator or Adam) sits upon a bench, dressed in a blue mantle over a reddish-brown tunic. He makes a gesture of speech or blessing with his right hand while holding a white scroll in his left hand that curls downward.

To the right of the figure and across the bottom half of the page are four registers of animals:
- Top register: A red lion, a bear, a stag, a blue stork-like bird, and a rabbit.
- Second register: A large tan dromedary camel and a blue donkey or horse.
- Third register: A large red ox or bull standing among stylized plants on the left, and a blue monkey or ape seated on a rocky mound on the right.
- Bottom register: A light blue ram, two red goats or deer, and a blue wolf or canine.
</image-desc>

--- Page 29 ---
<language>Latin</language>
<page-type>illustration</page-type>

<image-desc>
A full-page illuminated miniature from a medieval bestiary, divided into three horizontal registers against a burnished gold background. The entire miniature is enclosed in a decorative frame with a red, blue, and white geometric interlocking pattern.

1. **Top Register:** A large tan-colored lion stands over a smaller, primate-like creature (possibly a monkey). The lion is biting the smaller creature. Stylized trees with blue trunks and orange, leaf-like tops frame the scene.
2. **Middle Register:** On the left, a large red lion stands over a man who is lying prostrate on the ground. To the right, three men stand watching. They are dressed in tunics of blue, white, and red, carrying staves and bags over their shoulders. This scene likely illustrates the legendary mercy of the lion toward those who humble themselves.
3. **Bottom Register:** A lion on the right is bowing its head low toward a rooster on the left. The rooster has brown and blue plumage and a prominent red comb. More stylized blue and orange trees appear in the background. This scene likely illustrates the lore that the lion is terrified of the crowing of a white cock.
</image-desc>

<vocab>leo, bestiary, illumination, gallus, lion, rooster</vocab>

--- Page 30 ---
<language>Latin</language>
<page-type>illustration</page-type>

<image-desc>
A full-page medieval illumination from a bestiary, divided into three horizontal registers set against a gold leaf background. The entire scene is enclosed in a decorative rectangular border featuring a repeating pattern of white scrolling lines on a red and blue ground.

- **Top Register:** A large, stylized lion with a brown mane and body stands facing left on a series of stylized, multi-colored rocky mounds. The lion has wide eyes and a long tail that curls over its back. Stylized botanical elements with red and green bulbous ends are scattered around the animal.
- **Middle Register:** A blue lioness is depicted reclining inside a red, flame-like or wave-like stylized cavern. She is accompanied by two smaller blue cubs. 
- **Bottom Register:** This scene depicts the "Resurrection of the Cubs." A tan lion and a blue lion stand over three small, pale cubs lying on a patch of green ground. The blue lion is bent forward, breathing life into the cubs. In medieval bestiary tradition, this illustrated the belief that lion cubs were born dead and brought to life after three days by the breath of their father, symbolizing the Resurrection of Christ.
</image-desc>

<vocab>Bestiary, Leo, Resurrection, illumination, gold leaf, Physiologus</vocab>

--- Page 31 ---

sum;

<image-desc>A rectangular illuminated miniature with a burnished gold background, framed by a border of red, green, and blue. In the left circle, a man wearing a blue tunic and red hose thrusts a long spear or pole towards the right. In the right circle, a large mother ape walks on its hind legs, carrying a smaller ape on its back. <page-num>25</page-num>

<image-desc>
A full-page medieval manuscript illumination divided into three horizontal registers set against a burnished gold leaf background. The entire miniature is enclosed within a decorative border featuring blue and orange scrollwork and geometric patterns.

- **Top Register:** A knight in chainmail and a white surcoat, mounted on a light-colored horse with green trappings, charges from the left. He thrusts a long wooden lance into the chest of a man in a red tunic on the right, who falls back against a stylized green rocky outcrop. An orange dog stands over the fallen man, looking up at the knight.
- **Middle Register:** Against a background of stylized trees with orange and blue foliate clusters, two scenes are depicted. On the left, an orange dog is curled in a circle, sleeping or grieving. On the right, a man in a red tunic lies prostrate and lifeless over a blue burial mound, representing the grave of his master.
- **Bottom Register:** A narrative scene with multiple figures. On the left, a man in a white cloak and blue hat stands with a staff. Beside him, a man in a red tunic holds a large blue-grey dog. In the center, two men are grappling on the ground; one in a blue tunic pins another in a red tunic to the earth. On the far right, a man in a brown tunic points toward the struggle while holding a spear. This scene likely depicts the dog identifying its master's murderer in a crowd.
</image-desc>

<vocab>Bestiary, Canis, dog, loyalty, knight, lance, illumination, gold leaf, master, murder</vocab>

<image-desc>
A full-page medieval illumination divided into three horizontal registers, set against a solid gold-leaf background and enclosed within a decorative border of red and white quatrefoil and geometric motifs.

- **Top Register:** On the left, a seated figure (possibly a judge or ruler) in a reddish robe and white mantle holds a large, upright sword in his left hand while pointing with his right. In the center, a man in a tan tunic gestures towards the right. On the far right, a large blue wolf or hound leaps upon a man in an orange tunic, biting his shoulder; the man raises a wooden club or staff to strike the beast.

- **Middle Register:** Three men dressed in tunics of blue, red, and green stand amidst stylized, multi-colored trees. The man on the left holds a long staff or spear. All three figures are gesturing, appearing to be in mid-conversation or pointing towards the foliage.

- **Bottom Register:** Two men, one in a tan tunic and one in red, lean over a rectangular blue and red sarcophagus. They are carefully lowering a body wrapped in a reddish-brown shroud into the tomb. The face of the deceased is visible at the right end of the shroud.
</image-desc>

Dromedarius genus est camelorum, minoris quidem stature, sed velocior: unde nomen habet. Nam dromos grece cursus et velocitas appellatur; centum enim et amplius milia una die pergere solet. Quod animal sicut ovis et bos et camelus ruminat. Ruminatio autem dicta a ruma eminente gutturis parte per quam dimissos cibos a ceteris animalibus revocat;

<image-desc>A medieval miniature painting depicting a dark brown, donkey-like animal (meant to be a dromedary) with long ears and a white star on its shoulder. It stands against a gleaming gold-leaf background, enclosed in a rectangular frame with blue and red geometric decorative patterns.</image-desc>

## D E A S I N

Asinus et asellus a sedendo dicti quasi asedulus: si nomenque magis esset conveniebat. Ideo hoc animal sumptum quod postquam equos ceperunt homines; huic presidere ceperunt. Animal quoque tardum in ista rationem trahens, statim ut noluit sed homo substratur: Asinos arcadeos dictos, eo quod ab arcadia primum vecti sunt, magni vulti. Minor asellus: onagro plus necessarius est, quia et laborem tollerat: et negligentiam et penoclam inrecusat.

<image-desc>A medieval miniature painting showing an onager (wild ass) in a dynamic trotting pose with its head turned backwards. It is set against a gold-leaf background inside a decorative square frame featuring blue, red, and white geometric designs.</image-desc>

## D E O N A G

Onager interpretatur asinus ferus. oros quippe greci asinum vocant agriam ferum; hos assini tales habent. magnos in deserto vagantes. singuli autem feminarum gregibus presunt; Has tenent parvulis zelant et testiculos

## Qualiter accipiter plumescere debeat

Domesticis accipitribus quo melius plumescere debeant: munita ac repentina
requirunt loca. Loca munita sunt claustra. In quibus dum
siluestris accipiter ponitur: ut domesticus fiat. clausus tenetur.
ibi ueteres pennas emittit. et nouas assumit: quia quilibet clau
stralis quia pristinis uiciis spoliatur: noui hominis uirtute ad
ornatur. et dum in exitu: nisi prius eiectas ueteris pennas noue soli
dunt; sed cum firmi uolatu fuerit: eiecti foras. in manum uenit.
Similiter si aliquis econsilium de claustro exeat: necesse ut in manum
bone operationis accedat. et in emissus uolet: ut ad desideran
da celestia toto nisu mentis se ipsum leuet;

<margin>
De accipiter in sinistra manu gestatus
</margin>

Accipit in sinistra manu gestari solet. ut in dextram ad
aliquid capiendum emissus uolet. leua inquit ei
sub capite meo. et dextra illius amplexabit me. Leua: sunt
bona temporalia. dextra uero sunt eterna. In leua igitur sedet: qui bonis
temporalibus studet. In dextera uero uolat: qui toto affectu
mentis eterna desiderat. Ibi capit et accipit columbam: et qui
bet mutatus in melius: tali specie recipiet gratiam;

<image-desc>A square miniature set against a burnished gold leaf background. In the center is a complex circular frame with green, red, and blue borders. Within this frame is a quatrefoil pattern with segments of blue and pink background. Four reddish-brown birds, identified as hoopoes (epopus), are depicted in an arrangement: one bird is positioned centrally with another above it, and two birds are placed in the side lobes of the quatrefoil. The birds have long, thin beaks and are shown grooming or attending to one another, illustrating the theme of young birds caring for their elderly parents.</image-desc>

# De hirundine.

<image-desc>A square miniature illustration depicting a swallow in profile with dark plumage, a red throat, and a white underbelly. The main illustration is divided into two registers with a gold-leaf background:
- The upper register shows two eagles. On the left, a large eagle with reddish-brown plumage and white wing-tips flies over a stylized green, undulating hill, carrying a large blue fish in its talons. To its right, another eagle flies upward, gazing toward the sun.
- The lower register depicts an eagle plunging headfirst into a large, circular stone basin or font filled with green, wavy water. The basin is flanked by two stylized, curving floral stems with orange and blue blossoms.

<header>De Salamandra</header>

<image-desc>A large square illumination with a polished gold-leaf background, enclosed in a decorative patterned border. The miniature is divided into two horizontal registers. In the upper register, a stylized tree with symmetrical, swirling branches and multicolored buds stands in the center. ### De Lapidibus Ignitis.

<image-desc>A framed illumination with a gold background, divided into two horizontal registers. In the top register, a naked man and woman stand on stylized blue and orange mounds on opposite sides of a central tree, each holding a small green stone. In the bottom register, the same man and woman are depicted from the waist up, standing behind similar mounds that are engulfed in red and orange flames; they reach out to touch each other. The entire miniature is flanked by vertical borders of stylized colorful foliage.</image-desc>

<vocab>cadaver, exequias, defunctus, sepultus, humare, lapidibus ignitis, fire stones</vocab>

Sunt lapides igniferi in quodam monte ori-
entis qui grece dicuntur tirobolen. masculus et fe-
mina. Isti quando longe fuerint ab invicem ignis
in eis non accenditur; Cum autem casu appropinqua-
verit femina masculo: statim ignis accenditur: ita
ut ardeant omnia que sunt circa illum montem;
Unde et vos homines dei qui istam vitam geritis sepa-
rate vos longe a feminis ne cum appropinquaveri-
tis ad invicem: accendatur in vobis ille ignis ge-
minus. et consumat bona que Christus contulit in vobis. Si
enim angeli sathane qui semper impugnant iustos.
non solum sanctos viros. sed etiam feminas castas. Denique
samson et ioseph ambo per mulieres temptati sunt.
Unus vicit: alius victus est. Eva et susanna temptate
sunt. Si iuxta illa meta est custodiendum. tunc ergo cor-
da divinis preceptis omnimodis monendum. Nam
amor feminarum quarum peccatum ab initio cepit. et
ab adam usque nunc in filios inobediencie deva-
chatur,
